a2:["$","p",null,{"children":["On a planar lattice, a flow set is a directed path (or a union of vertex-disjoint directed paths) through the interaction graph, and finding a small set of such paths that covers every edge becomes a combinatorial problem in its own right, rather than a two-line classification.
